2026-10-17T16:50:31.3040150Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-17T16:50:31.3079397Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Record Prefill MegaMoE expert counts under the official workload

Statistics-only run on the measured image e29dfde4... with main's runtime
(reference run 38945855851: 6521.98 good output tok/s, c192, P3/D1, every
worker TP4/DP4/EP4, MegaMoE on both roles). The score of this run is not
an adoption candidate; it exists to measure expert-parallel load
imbalance on the Prefill workers.

Motivation: the Prefill MegaMoE kernel averages 2.17 ms per layer in
serving, while the pre-serving gate (check-firmus-megamoe.py, balanced
random routing at 4096 tokens per rank) measures 1.66 ms, and Prefill
chunks are full (mean ~4028 tokens per rank). The fused kernel waits for
the slowest EP rank, so a systematic per-rank token skew under real
routing is one candidate for the missing ~0.5 ms/layer. A static
permutation of the 128 experts over the 4 EP ranks (SGLang
--init-expert-location with zero redundant experts) is precision-neutral
and can remove a systematic skew, but only if one exists; this run
measures it before any build.

Changes (Prefill only; decode is untouched):
- --expert-distribution-recorder-mode stat with a 2048-pass circular
  buffer. The MegaMoE a2a backend uses SGLang's select-experts gatherer,
  which counts topk ids per layer and forward pass; one official client
  run is ~1200 target plus draft passes per rank, so nothing wraps.
- SGLANG_EXPERT_DISTRIBUTION_RECORDER_DIR points rank 0's dump into
  results/expert-distribution/<worker>, which the workflow collects.
- Recording starts on the three Prefill workers right before the
  official client and stops right after it, so it spans warmup,
  measurement and grace (the client's phases are not separable from the
  server side without timing guesses). The dump all-reduces each pass
  over the worker's four EP ranks, giving (passes, 94 layers, 128
  experts) token-assignment counts; rank r owns experts 32r..32r+31 under
  the current trivial placement.

Analysis after the run (no GPU): per-pass, per-layer rank load max/mean
under the trivial placement, the max/mean after a balanced permutation,
and the per-expert skew. If the trivial max/mean averaged over layers is
below ~1.05 the placement experiment stops there.

Shell syntax, the line-continuation check, ruff and the full test suite
pass locally.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

imbalance on the Prefill workers.

Motivation: the Prefill MegaMoE kernel averages 2.17 ms per layer in
serving, while the pre-serving gate (check-firmus-megamoe.py, balanced
random routing at 4096 tokens per rank) measures 1.66 ms, and Prefill
chunks are full (mean ~4028 tokens per rank). The fused kernel waits for
the slowest EP rank, so a systematic per-rank token skew under real
routing is one candidate for the missing ~0.5 ms/layer. A static
permutation of the 128 experts over the 4 EP ranks (SGLang
--init-expert-location with zero redundant experts) is precision-neutral
a
